# rq6-xlmr-dev

RQ6, the two model families against the opponent on development data, three seeds
each: the XLM-RoBERTa large token classifier (train-cs-sk-xlmr-ce, at its default 0.5
and at 0.2) and GLiNER fine-tuned with cross-entropy and focal loss (--tag gpu), all
trained on MetaCentrum (fobos, NVIDIA L40S), against NameTag 3 multilingual (the
headline opponent), NameTag 3 Czech and GLiNER zero-shot. Only the corpora of real
text, where the final test is decided (OpenPII is saturated at 0.99 for every trained
model and costs NameTag hours on a CPU). Train the XLM-R models first
(docs/metacentrum.md); NameTag 3 must be stored in the resource root.
